# Source construct catalog

Generated by `tools/build_coverage.py`. The fixed diagram population and supplementary named dictionary constructs are tagged separately. Native SDK shapes have their own appendix and are not added to entity counts.

## AWS Health

### health:Account — Account

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Supplied external AWS account context for memberships and event impact. Health did not supply a complete account directory.

**Identity.** String AWS account ID qualified by partition where applicable.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACCOUNT`.

**Aliases.** `Account`, `ACCOUNT`.

**Target entities.** `Workspace`, `Connection`, `Resource`, `EventContext`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/OwningScope/properties/accountId`; `#/$defs/OwningScope/properties/partition`; `#/$defs/EventContext/properties/accountId`; `#/$defs/OrganizationMembership/properties/accountId`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Account identity is a typed scope/reference value. It deliberately does not become a competing account-directory entity; names and organizational enrichment remain externally sourced.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-18](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-18), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/health/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/health/analysis/report.md#3-core-entity-dictionary-and-keys)
- [aws/health/analysis/diagrams/01-core-model.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/health/analysis/diagrams/01-core-model.mmd) — `ACCOUNT`

### health:AggregateSnapshot — AggregateSnapshot

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Cached an aggregate answer with exact query scope, requested breakdown, counting unit, status map and completeness.

**Identity.** Scoped query/as-of capture, not a mutable health entity.

**Original declaration.** Supplementary named construct discussed in the source report; it is not in the fixed class/ER-diagram denominator.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `AggregateSnapshot`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.health.aggregate/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The named console.health.aggregate source profile retains an application-origin capture. It explicitly adds no typed Projection profile or aggregate result schema.

### health:CollectionItemError — CollectionItemError

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Represented failedSet entries tied to the failed event/account even when the overall transport response was HTTP 200.

**Identity.** Collection/read + specific event/account item.

**Original declaration.** Supplementary named construct discussed in the source report; it is not in the fixed class/ER-diagram denominator.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `CollectionItemError`.

**Target entities.** `ReadAttempt`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ReadAttempt/properties/error`; `#/$defs/ReadError`; `#/$defs/ReadAttempt/properties/responseSource`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.event/v1`; `aws.health.entity-impact/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Raw response evidence can preserve failedSet. ReadAttempt.error is one ReadError with code/message/retryable/request ID and has neither a collection of item errors nor event/account item identity.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Add a typed owned item-error collection or a typed response-profile binding that preserves item subject scope and partial-success semantics; do not invent extra native read attempts for failed batch items.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/health/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/health/analysis/report.md#12-coverage-and-collection-model)

### health:CommunicationPage — CommunicationPage

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Represented a logical page and its total-pages context; repeated or backup deliveries did not create new logical pages.

**Identity.** Communication grouping + exact raw communication ID + page number, with conflicting payload candidates retained.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Aliases.** `CommunicationPage`, `COMMUNICATION_PAGE`.

**Target entities.** `Communication`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Communication/properties/pages`; `#/$defs/CommunicationPage`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Communication owns typed page candidates with raw ID, pageNumber, totalPages, digest and exact delivery observations. Page changes use the owning Communication version.

### health:EntityImpact — EntityImpact

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Represented an event-relative affected construct, including groups and broad/unknown markers, with its own update clock and optional verified inventory matches.

**Identity.** Local impact ID within EventContext; optional provider Health entity ARN; a bare entityValue is not globally unique.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `ENTITY_IMPACT`.

**Aliases.** `EntityImpact`, `ENTITY_IMPACT`.

**Target entities.** `EntityImpact`, `EventContext`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/EntityImpact`; `#/$defs/EntityImpact/properties/eventContextId`; `#/$defs/EntityImpact/properties/healthEntityArn`; `#/$defs/EntityImpact/properties/selectedObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.entity-impact/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The normalized impact preserves contextual ownership, optional native identity, raw provider status and exact observation. It remains separate from inventory Resource and does not infer global health from event-relative state.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### health:EntityResourceMatch — EntityResourceMatch

**PARTIAL** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Supported zero, one or several verified links from an event-relative affected construct to external inventory, with method, confidence and evidence.

**Identity.** Impact/resource match under the owning evidence and rule.

**Original declaration.** Supplementary named construct discussed in the source report; it is not in the fixed class/ER-diagram denominator.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `EntityResourceMatch`.

**Target entities.** `EntityImpact`, `Resource`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/EntityImpact/properties/resourceMatches`; `#/$defs/ResourceMatch`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ResourceMatch supplies MATCHED/AMBIGUOUS/UNMATCHED, ruleDigest and exact evidence, but it does not carry the explicitly described match-method label or confidence value.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Document how the ruleDigest resolves method/confidence, or add the minimum owned qualification if these source semantics remain product requirements.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### health:EventDescription — EventDescription

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Localized description text belonged to an exact contextual event revision, not a globally mutable incident text field.

**Identity.** Event revision + locale.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EVENT_DESCRIPTION`.

**Aliases.** `EventDescription`, `EVENT_DESCRIPTION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/EventDescription`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Raw detail content can be retained through the event profile. EventDescription is declared in $defs but is not referenced by any entity, value owner or profile discriminator; the declared type alone is not a normalized description binding.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Bind EventDescription to its actual contextual Source/profile or an owning EventContext value if normalized locale/text retrieval is required. Preserve absence and locale-request provenance.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### health:EventRevision — EventRevision

**NORMALIZED** · `HISTORY_REPRESENTATION` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Retained an immutable contextual event representation and field-presence information, so a new summary did not erase hydrated detail.

**Identity.** Local revision tied to an EventContext and source observation; it was not a provider revision ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EVENT_REVISION`.

**Aliases.** `EventRevision`, `EVENT_REVISION`.

**Target entities.** `EventContext`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/EventContext/properties/version`; `#/$defs/EventContext/properties/selectedObservation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/version`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/fieldPresence`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The history role maps to ordinary EventContext and Source versions plus an exact Observation, without retaining a version-only entity. Provider, local publication and observation clocks stay separate.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-10](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-10), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### health:EventType — EventType

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Catalog classification supplied type code, service, category, actionability and personas without overriding classifications observed on an event.

**Identity.** Provider type code qualified by service/source scope.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EVENT_TYPE`.

**Aliases.** `EventType`, `EVENT_TYPE`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.event-type/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** A named EventType source profile retains the catalog payload. There is no bound normalized EventType value or entity; inspect the retained source when this catalog breadth is needed.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### health:HealthEvent — HealthEvent

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** The stable event identity tied event type, service, scope and impacted geography together without multiplying it by account context or notification delivery.

**Identity.** Full provider event ARN; contextual status and detail belong to scoped evidence.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `HEALTH_EVENT`.

**Aliases.** `HealthEvent`, `HEALTH_EVENT`.

**Target entities.** `HealthEvent`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/HealthEvent`; `#/$defs/HealthEvent/properties/eventArn`; `#/$defs/HealthEvent/properties/selectedSource`; `#/$defs/Source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.health.event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** HealthEvent provides a scoped canonical identity, provider ARN and typed classification/index fields with an exact selected Source. Provider detail fields remain retained evidence rather than additional event identities.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### health:LifecycleExposure — LifecycleExposure

**PARTIAL** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Assessed whether a deployment was affected by a particular milestone, preserving rule/policy version, confidence and confirmed/candidate/excluded/unknown applicability.

**Identity.** Deployment/milestone/rule/cutoff interpretation; it is not a native Health status.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `LIFECYCLE_EXPOSURE`.

**Aliases.** `LifecycleExposure`, `LIFECYCLE_EXPOSURE`.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `ComponentDeployment`, `LifecycleEntry`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Projection/allOf/1/then/properties/value`; `#/$defs/LifecycleExposure`; `#/$defs/LifecycleExposure/properties/applicability`; `#/$defs/LifecycleExposure/properties/matchingRuleDigest`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.health.lifecycle-exposure/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** LIFECYCLE_EXPOSURE is a real typed projection with exact evidence, matching rule, reason and policy snapshot. The source model also distinguishes candidate matching and confidence; current applicability collapses to APPLIES/DOES_NOT_APPLY/UNKNOWN and has no confidence field.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- State the deliberate candidate-to-UNKNOWN mapping and where confidence is retained, or add the smallest owned match qualification needed by the product; no new exposure entity is required.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-09](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-09), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/health/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/health/analysis/report.md#assessment-and-matching)

**Previous use.** Recorded the observed status of Health organization integration independently of desired enable/disable intent and backfill completeness.

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Identity.** Local work-item ID with optimistic concurrency.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `WORK_ITEM`.

**Aliases.** `WorkItem`, `WORK_ITEM`.

**Target entities.** `WorkItem`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/WorkItem`; `#/$defs/WorkItem/properties/version`; `#/$defs/WorkItem/properties/owner`; `#/$defs/WorkItem/properties/verificationState`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** WorkItem owns typed state, assignment, timing, targets and verification with one entity version. Local completion remains distinct from provider confirmation.

### health:WorkItemTarget — WorkItemTarget

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Linked coordinated work to exactly one context, impact or exposure per target.

**Identity.** Work item + typed target; a separate target table was an optional physical implementation.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

## AWS Resilience Hub

### resilience-hub:AssessmentEntity — AssessmentEntity

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Tracked native service-scoped assessment execution and diagnostics separately from posture, current findings and remediation verification.

**Identity.** Service scope + native assessment ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `ASSESSMENT`.

**Aliases.** `AssessmentEntity`, `ASSESSMENT`.

**Target entities.** `ResilienceAssessment`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ResilienceAssessment`; `#/$defs/ResilienceAssessment/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/ResilienceAssessment/properties/rawExecutionStatus`; `#/$defs/ResilienceAssessment/properties/selectedObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.assessment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** ResilienceAssessment has substantive identity and typed execution/context references. Additional native diagnostics, costs and component outcomes remain available through retained source profiles; SUCCESS is not a health or remediation verdict.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Coverage judgment.** Projection owns the stable view identity, exact inputs, rules, cutoff and coverage, with enforced ServiceStatus output for the service-status profile. Its entity version replaces the source-only projectionRevision counter.

### resilience-hub:DependencyDiscoveryConfig — DependencyDiscoveryConfig

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Explained dependency-discovery initialization/enablement, eligible resource count, diagnostic text and provider update time.

**Identity.** One optional observed configuration value per native Service.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `DEPENDENCY_DISCOVERY_CONFIG`.

**Aliases.** `DependencyDiscoveryConfig`, `DEPENDENCY_DISCOVERY_CONFIG`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Coverage judgment.** The native Service profile retains dependencyDiscovery. Canonical Service has no typed discovery-config property, and aggregate ServiceStatus.discovery does not encode this config/count/message structure. The registry ownedValue label is not a typed owner binding.

### resilience-hub:DependencyGovernance — DependencyGovernance

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Recorded a product allowed-status decision independently of provider criticality and DNS observations; it did not grant network access.

**Identity.** Owned local decision under the same scoped Dependency.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

### resilience-hub:EffectivePolicyEvidence — EffectivePolicyEvidence

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** The earlier facade returned effective-target observation and policy-context capture together so clients could inspect the targets and associations behind posture.

**Identity.** Composite read view over a same-service target observation and policy capture.

**Original declaration.** Supplementary named construct discussed in the source report; it is not in the fixed class/ER-diagram denominator.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `EffectivePolicyEvidence`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/PolicyContext`; `#/$defs/ServiceStatus/properties/policyContextSnapshot`; `#/$defs/PolicyContext/properties/effectiveTargetObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** The typed policy snapshot and exact references preserve the composition and historical context. The selected effective-target content remains opaque, so the former inline target values are not fully normalized.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- PolicyContext pins an effective-target Source and Observation, but no schema binds the target payload to the eight metrics with per-value units, policy-name and ownership-source provenance. The former inline effectivePolicyEvidence view is therefore only partially normalized.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### resilience-hub:EffectiveTarget — EffectiveTarget

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Preserved one returned metric/value/unit and its optional policy name and account-ownership source, without inventing a policy ARN or inheritance relation.

**Identity.** Effective-target observation + metric.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EFFECTIVE_TARGET`.

**Aliases.** `EffectiveTarget`, `EFFECTIVE_TARGET`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

- The profile calls provenance policyType while the source report distinguishes ownership source SELF/CROSS_ACCOUNT from assignment origin. Reconcile names and typed semantics against the pinned source before normalization.

### resilience-hub:EffectiveTargetObservation — EffectiveTargetObservation

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Captured the effective targets returned for one Service, including an observation clock and exact source evidence.

**Identity.** Service-scoped local observation ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EFFECTIVE_TARGET_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `EffectiveTargetObservation`, `EFFECTIVE_TARGET_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Observation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/PolicyContext/properties/effectiveTargetObservation`; `#/$defs/PolicyContext/properties/effectiveTargetSource`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.effective-targets/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Observation supplies a timed occurrence with an exact Source reference, while same-service PolicyContext binds effectiveTargetObservation and effectiveTargetSource. Together they preserve the service-scoped effective-target observation envelope and historical selection without a new observation subtype. The untyped child metric values are assessed separately under EffectiveTarget; they do not make the envelope itself unmapped or partial.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### resilience-hub:EntityRef — EntityRef

**PARTIAL** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Represented typed, scoped provider subjects, including unresolved identities and tombstones, so historical events remained resolvable.

**Identity.** Entity type + verified provider scope/key; old local entityKey indexed that tuple.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `ENTITY_REF`.

**Aliases.** `EntityRef`, `ENTITY_REF`.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

### resilience-hub:Event — Event

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Retained channel-scoped domain/API timeline event envelopes and distinct occurred/received clocks as evidence and invalidation signals.

**Identity.** Source channel + stream owner/scope + source event ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EVENT`.

**Aliases.** `Event`, `EVENT`, `Event envelope`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/sourceOperation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/representation`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/channel`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/sourceTime`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/transportTime`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Source identity/profile/operation and Observation occurrence preserve event evidence and separate clocks. The raw event remains inspectable; event receipt does not prove an asynchronous workflow completed. Subject-link normalization is assessed separately.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/resilience-hub/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/resilience-hub/analysis/report.md#12-temporal-model-for-the-operator-view)
- [aws/resilience-hub/analysis/diagrams/05-events-observations.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/resilience-hub/analysis/diagrams/05-events-observations.md) — `EVENT`

### resilience-hub:EventSubject — EventSubject

**PARTIAL** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Linked one event to typed subjects with role, asserted/inferred relationship and linkage evidence.

**Identity.** Event + scoped subject + role.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `EVENT_SUBJECT`.

**Aliases.** `EventSubject`, `EVENT_SUBJECT`, `Event subjects`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Native envelope resource/account references can be retained, but neither Source nor Observation has a typed event-subject collection with role and linkage provenance. General Projection.subjectRefs are not an equivalent event-owned relationship.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Add or explicitly defer a typed owned event-subject binding with role and evidence. Do not claim normalized subject-link coverage solely from raw envelope resources.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

### resilience-hub:FindingCapture — FindingCapture

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Captured a scoped finding enumeration with separate list/detail coverage and an optional same-service assessment correlation plus its basis.

**Identity.** Local capture ID under Service, with exact query/interval; assessment relation was correlation only.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `FINDING_CAPTURE`.

**Aliases.** `FindingCapture`, `FINDING_CAPTURE`.

**Target entities.** `Collection`, `ReadAttempt`, `Finding`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Collection`; `#/$defs/CollectionQuery`; `#/$defs/Coverage`; `#/$defs/Finding/properties/currentCaptureCollection`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Collection/ReadAttempt/Coverage normalize the query, attempts, intervals and list/detail dimensions. There is no explicit typed related-assessment plus correlationBasis value; using generic query filters or a generic subject alone would not preserve that qualified relation.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Declare how optional assessment correlation is encoded and validated under Collection or a typed capture value. Keep it explicitly non-authoritative; do not add an Assessment→Finding ownership foreign key.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/resilience-hub/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/resilience-hub/analysis/report.md#reviewed-capture-identity-and-detail-coverage)

### resilience-hub:FindingEntity — FindingEntity

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Tracked a current Service-scoped finding with disposition, severity and independently hydrated detail, without an authoritative assessment-to-finding foreign key.

**Identity.** Service scope + native finding ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `FINDING`.

**Aliases.** `FindingEntity`, `FINDING`.

**Target entities.** `Finding`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Finding`; `#/$defs/Finding/properties/serviceId`; `#/$defs/Finding/properties/selectedSummaryObservation`; `#/$defs/Finding/properties/selectedDetailObservation`; `#/$defs/Finding/properties/recommendationPresence`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.finding/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Finding preserves scoped identity, raw status/severity, separate summary/detail selection, collection context and recommendation presence. It does not invent assessment ownership.

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Held one immutable summary or detail representation of a finding within a capture; failed hydration did not erase prior known detail.

**Identity.** Capture + Service + Finding + representation, with a local observation ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `FINDING_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `FindingObservation`, `FINDING_OBSERVATION`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.finding/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Source representation/profile/operation identity and exact Observation/read-attempt links preserve the summary/detail distinction and capture provenance. Finding selects those exact records.

### resilience-hub:HealthObservation — HealthObservation

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Retained independently sourced runtime telemetry state with signal identity, measurement window, measurement/receipt/expiry times and evidence.

**Identity.** Subject + signal/source + observation/window, independent of resilience assessment execution.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `HEALTH_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `HealthObservation`, `HEALTH_OBSERVATION`, `Health observation`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/observedAt`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/expiresAt`; `#/$defs/ServiceStatus/properties/runtimeHealth`; `#/$defs/StateDimension`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.monitoring.health/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Observation supplies typed receipt and expiry metadata, and StateDimension supplies a result badge with evidence. The console.monitoring.health payload is opaque; signal identity, measured window and typed measurements are not a normalized evidence contract.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Keep this as an explicitly retained telemetry profile until the first product use case selects a typed observation contract; do not invent a generic telemetry entity for completeness alone.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### resilience-hub:PolicyComponent — PolicyComponent

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Held up to four typed policy families with defined numeric targets, units and disaster-recovery approach.

**Identity.** Policy + component type; no independent AWS lifecycle.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `POLICY_COMPONENT`.

**Aliases.** `PolicyComponent`, `POLICY_COMPONENT`.

**Target entities.** `ResiliencePolicy`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ResiliencePolicy/properties/components`; `#/$defs/PolicyComponent`; `#/$defs/PolicyComponent/properties/targets`; `#/$defs/PolicyComponent/properties/units`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ResiliencePolicy.components binds PolicyComponent with a known component family, numeric targets, explicit units and an optional disaster-recovery approach. This represents the source component meaning as an owner-versioned value. The RPC ResiliencePolicyChanges and RecoveryTargetsInput shapes additionally specify native edit metrics and bounds. Open read-map keys and looser metric/unit/integer checks are validation-completeness concerns, not a missing representation.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

### resilience-hub:PostureObservation — PostureObservation

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Distinguished native service component achievability from derived system/journey attention summaries, retaining captured context, contributors, coverage and rule identity.

**Identity.** Scoped subject + local observation; optional assessment association was qualified correlation.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `POSTURE_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `PostureObservation`, `POSTURE_OBSERVATION`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.posture/v1`; `console.resilience.status/v1`.

### resilience-hub:RecommendationEnrichment — RecommendationEnrichment

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Added local title, estimate, complexity and provenance around a captured suggestion; operational work could be held separately.

**Identity.** Owned by a recommendation instance; older source overlay had its own version counter.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `RECOMMENDATION_ENRICHMENT`.

**Aliases.** `RecommendationEnrichment`, `RECOMMENDATION_ENRICHMENT`.

**Target entities.** `Recommendation`, `WorkItem`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Recommendation/properties/enrichment`; `#/$defs/RecommendationEnrichment`; `#/$defs/RecommendationEstimate`; `#/$defs/WorkItem`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Recommendation owns typed enrichment/estimate values under its one entity version. Assignment, work state and verification belong to WorkItem; no independent enrichment version remains.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### resilience-hub:RecommendationEntity — RecommendationEntity

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Preserved each generated recommendation category/ordinal and its ordered suggestedChanges without pretending the provider supplied an independent recommendation ID.

**Identity.** Local identity scoped to captured detail evidence + category + source-array ordinal.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `RECOMMENDATION`.

**Aliases.** `RecommendationEntity`, `RECOMMENDATION`.

**Target entities.** `Recommendation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Recommendation`; `#/$defs/Recommendation/properties/source`; `#/$defs/Recommendation/properties/groupKey`; `#/$defs/Recommendation/properties/ordinal`; `#/$defs/Recommendation/properties/suggestion`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.resilience-hub.recommendation/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Recommendation has a deliberate local identity, exact detail/source context and owned enrichment, but suggestion is unrestricted JsonValue. The native ordered suggestedChanges shape is documented only by a source-profile hint.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Bind the retained suggestion to a typed owned recommendation-content shape if normalized rendering/comparison is promised. Preserve category, grouping and ordering; do not equate an ordinal across changed source versions.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### resilience-hub:TopologyEdge — TopologyEdge

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Preserved a same-capture directed edge and every native property, including inferred-label provenance, without implying outage propagation.

**Identity.** Topology capture + local edge ID; both node endpoints belong to that capture.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a named ER/class construct in the original diagram; that notation does not imply an independent AWS resource or a required target entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `TOPOLOGY_EDGE`.

**Aliases.** `TopologyEdge`, `TOPOLOGY_EDGE`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/GraphContents/properties/edges`; `#/$defs/GraphEdge`; `#/$defs/GraphEdge/properties/relationKind`; `#/$defs/GraphEdge/properties/claimKind`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.resilience.topology/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The typed edge preserves endpoint keys, one relationKind, claimKind and exact evidence. It has no owned properties[]/label collection, so the source model’s multiple native EdgeProperty values are not normalized.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Specify a lossless property-to-edge expansion/grouping rule or add a small owned property collection. Preserve native type, optional LLM-inferred label and source grouping; no standalone edge entity is needed.

### devops-agent:Accountusage — Account usage

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `TRANSPORT_DTO` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Account-level usage-window counters and limits for investigation/evaluation/system learning/on-demand/grading; not per-incident telemetry or recovery evidence.

**Identity.** Owning account + usage-period start/end.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.account-usage/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The account-usage profile retains typed native counter descriptions inside Source. The model has no normalized UsageMetric/account-usage value and does not conflate it with workload status.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-14](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-14), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:ActionObservation — ActionObservation

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An independently sourced observation of request, approval, resume, tool execution or effect; permission or credential redemption alone does not prove success.

**Identity.** Application observation/rendition ID with exact request/command/source context and stage.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: ACTION_OBSERVATION.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACTION_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_OBSERVATION`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.action-observation/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The named LOCAL_EVIDENCE source profile retains an action-stage rendition. No normalized ActionObservation value or Projection profile exists; generic Observation denotes receipt occurrence, not this stage/outcome view.

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Identity.** Owning partition/account/Region + agentSpaceId; ARN is an alias.

**Diagram symbols.** `SPACE`.

**Aliases.** `SPACE`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.agent-space/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** AgentSpace provides a typed scoped entity and association collection with exact selected observation. Peripheral provider configuration remains in its retained Source.

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Native resume/context value contains optional toolUseId, interruptId, approvalId, buttonText and action; it correlates the precise paused invocation.

**Identity.** Owned invocation/decision value scoped by AgentSpace and exact request context.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

### devops-agent:ApprovalPattern — ApprovalPattern

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Native requested/finalized approval scope consists of a tool and pinned argument map; finalization may match or narrow the request, never widen it.

**Identity.** Owned scope value for the exact approval request/decision context.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `ApprovalRequest`, `Command`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/requestSource`; `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/actionDigest`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.approval/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Approval context and digest are modeled, but the tool/argumentPins shape has no model definition or direct typed member. It remains in retained source content for adapter validation.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No ApprovalPattern or equivalent owned typed scope shape exists despite the legacy ownedValue/typed-pins label.

### devops-agent:ApprovalRequest — ApprovalRequest

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An observed paused-invocation request ties an approval ID to exact execution, interrupt/tool-use IDs and requested tool/argument scope; it is a validated open-content profile, not a universal native ApprovalRequest structure.

**Identity.** Scoped AgentSpace + approval ID and invocation context; request identity must not be guessed from prose or button labels.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: APPROVAL_REQUEST.

**Diagram symbols.** `APPROVAL_REQUEST`.

**Aliases.** `APPROVAL_REQUEST`.

**Target entities.** `ApprovalRequest`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest`; `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/requestSource`; `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/actionDigest`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.approval/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** ApprovalRequest normalizes execution, nativeApprovalId, state, exact request source, action digest and decision/resume command links. Invocation interrupt/tool-use IDs and the requested pattern remain inside Source.payload, with descriptor-level profile hints.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No typed model fields for interruptId, toolUseId or requested ApprovalPattern; the application must validate the retained request profile before commands.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:ArtifactReference — ArtifactReference

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Application discovery/presentation reference spanning native Asset files, journal/stream inline or external content and A2A artifacts without equating their IDs.

**Identity.** Source channel + exact record/block/locator + optional native artifact or Asset-version identity.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: ARTIFACT_REF.

**Diagram symbols.** `ARTIFACT_REF`.

**Aliases.** `ARTIFACT_REF`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Asset`, `Snapshot`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ArtifactRef`; `#/$defs/AssetFile`; `#/$defs/EvidenceRef`; `#/$defs/EvidenceLocator`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ArtifactRef supplies a managed durable locator, content version/digest and availability, while AssetFile and EvidenceRef supply related context. There is no one normalized value carrying the prior source-channel/record/block/native-ID/optional-asset-resolution contract.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- A2A versus journal versus native Asset identity and inline/external resolution are not explicit ArtifactRef members.
- Availability values collapse several source distinctions such as not-yet-published, access-denied, unsupported and deleted.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

### devops-agent:Asset — Asset

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Native AgentSpace asset with open type, metadata, versioned content and owned files; an asset in the same space is not automatically an investigation input or output.

**Identity.** Owning scope + AgentSpace + assetId.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: ASSET.

**Diagram symbols.** `ASSET`.

**Aliases.** `ASSET`.

**Target entities.** `Asset`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Asset`; `#/$defs/Asset/properties/nativeVersion`; `#/$defs/Asset/properties/source`; `#/$defs/Asset/properties/files`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.asset/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Asset has stable identity and ordinary entity versions with separate nativeVersion, exact source and files. Provider metadata stays retained; no inferred investigation-to-asset relation is introduced.

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A file belongs to an exact native Asset version and exact path, with text/bytes content, metadata and timestamps retained from the native response.

**Identity.** Scoped Asset + native Asset version + exact case-sensitive path; no independent file version counter.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: ASSET_FILE.

**Aliases.** `ASSET_FILE`, `AssetFileVersion`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.asset/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** AssetFile is a typed owned path/source/artifact value under the selected Asset representation. Raw body union and native file metadata remain source-backed, and the Asset nativeVersion supplies ownership.

**NORMALIZED** · `HISTORY_REPRESENTATION` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A specific native asset version used for reproducible metadata/content/file reads, distinct from an unversioned latest read.

### devops-agent:Association — Association

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A native space-scoped integration association carries its own ID, configuration discriminator and optional registered-service relation. Built-in serviceId aws does not require a registration.

**Identity.** Owning AgentSpace + associationId; serviceId is not unique within the space.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: ASSOCIATION.

**Diagram symbols.** `ASSOCIATION`.

**Aliases.** `ASSOCIATION`.

**Target entities.** `AgentSpace`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/AgentSpace/properties/associations`; `#/$defs/AgentAssociation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.association/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** AgentAssociation is a typed owned value in each AgentSpace version with associationId, serviceId, configurationKind, optional registration identity and exact source. Demotion to an owned value preserves its source identity; native configuration remains Source-backed.

### devops-agent:CollectionPass — CollectionPass

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** One bounded investigation reconciliation attempt with start/end, fixed queries, collector version and selection policy; owns read attempts including empty/failed reads.

### devops-agent:ConnectionContext — ConnectionContext

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Application access path plus authorization context qualifies collection, caching and historical exports without changing native identity.

**Identity.** Application connection ID + authorization-context identity.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Connection`, `ReadAttempt`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Connection`; `#/$defs/Connection/properties/authorizationContextKey`; `#/$defs/ReadAttempt/properties/authorizationContextKey`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/authorizationContextKey`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Connection and exact connection references preserve the access path, while attempts/observations retain the authorization-context key. Historical retrieval is checked against current authority.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#identity-and-scope)

### devops-agent:CoverageClaim — CoverageClaim

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A bounded claim about what one pass established for a named query, based on page-chain/read-attempt evidence and evaluation policy; exhaustion is not global completeness.

**Identity.** Owning collection/projection/capture version + query/window/scope + policy and supporting attempts.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: COVERAGE_CLAIM.

**Diagram symbols.** `COVERAGE_CLAIM`.

**Aliases.** `COVERAGE_CLAIM`, `CollectionCoverage`.

**Target entities.** `Collection`, `Projection`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Coverage`; `#/$defs/Collection/properties/coverage`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/coverage`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/coverage`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Coverage normalizes independent pagination/detail/resolution/identity/claim dimensions, exact attempts, scope/window, evaluation time and rationale. It does not carry an explicit evaluation-policy identity/version, particularly when owned by Collection or Snapshot.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Coverage has rationale but no typed policy/rule version; only Projection has ruleDigest at its owner level.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#collection-observations-are-separate-from-source-revisions)
- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/diagrams/D07-collection-observations.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/diagrams/D07-collection-observations.mmd) — `COVERAGE_CLAIM`

### devops-agent:DecisionAttempt — DecisionAttempt

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Records a distinct approval/rejection submission with requested action, finalized scope hash, actor and definitive or ambiguous outcome; decision is separate from resume.

**Identity.** Application decision intent/attempt identity scoped to the exact approval request.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: DECISION_ATTEMPT.

**Diagram symbols.** `DECISION_ATTEMPT`.

**Aliases.** `DECISION_ATTEMPT`.

**Target entities.** `Command`, `ApprovalRequest`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Command`; `#/$defs/CommandAttempt`; `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/decisionCommandIds`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Command and CommandAttempt normalize durable identity, authenticated actor, request digest, dispatch/reconcile state and observed evidence. DecideApproval supplies typed decision input, but the readable Command representation lacks an exact retained request/decision field.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- A requestDigest identifies intent but does not reconstruct the original APPROVED/REJECTED action or finalized pattern; no Command.requestSource/request value is exposed.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

### devops-agent:Derivation — Derivation

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** The parser and rule/model version that produced a derived rendition, with explicit distinction between native fields, deterministic parsing, operator assertions and inference.

**Identity.** Owner-local immutable derivation identity/version; a new parser must not rewrite an old result.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: DERIVATION.

**Diagram symbols.** `DERIVATION`.

**Aliases.** `DERIVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Projection/properties/profileVersion`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/ruleDigest`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Projection retains profileVersion and ruleDigest, which identify the selected computation. The source contract’s explicit derivation kind/assertion origin and separate parser/model metadata are not a typed owned Derivation shape.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No typed normalized assertion-origin/derivation-kind member distinguishes direct provider mapping, operator statement and application inference.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:DerivedRevision — DerivedRevision

**PARTIAL** · `HISTORY_REPRESENTATION` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Immutable rendition of a logical timeline item, finding, result document or graph assertion, retaining item kind, assertion origin, payload and derivation identity.

**Identity.** Stable logical item + immutable rendition; old model used projectionRevisionId separately.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: DERIVED_REVISION.

**Diagram symbols.** `DERIVED_REVISION`.

**Aliases.** `DERIVED_REVISION`.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Projection/properties/version`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/ruleDigest`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.timeline/v1`; `console.investigation.assertion/v1`; `console.investigation.action-observation/v1`; `console.investigation.result/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Ordinary Projection versions normalize five result profiles. Other DevOps renditions are retained as application-origin Source documents; the generic prior item-kind/assertion-origin contract is not a normalized Projection variant.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Projection.profile is closed to five variants; timeline/assertion/action-observation variants do not exist.
- The normalized Projection envelope has no assertionOrigin field.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-10](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-10), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

### devops-agent:Eventnotification — Event notification

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `TRANSPORT_DTO` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** A separately sourced EventBridge lifecycle event supplies phase, event time, task/execution links and sometimes an exact summary pointer; it is not a journal entry.

**Identity.** Event channel + account/Region + native event ID.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/channel`; `#/$defs/InvestigationResult/properties/completionObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.lifecycle-event/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The lifecycle-event profile retains the full envelope and Observation carries channel/time. InvestigationResult normalizes selected completion-pointer meaning, not the complete lifecycle-event DTO.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-14](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-14), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:GraphEdge — GraphEdge

**PARTIAL** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Snapshot-owned typed relation with same-snapshot endpoints and evidence; parentage, support/contradiction, temporal order, dependency and asserted causality retain different meaning.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: GRAPH_EDGE.

**Diagram symbols.** `GRAPH_EDGE`.

**Aliases.** `GRAPH_EDGE`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/GraphContents/properties/edges`; `#/$defs/GraphEdge`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.graph/v1`.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Operator-confirmed versus independently observed versus application-inferred provenance and optional published confidence/disposition are not normalized edge fields.

### devops-agent:GraphNode — GraphNode

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Snapshot-owned graph node with kind, exact source/projection references, label, assertion origin, validity/observation context and explicit unresolved-placeholder state.

### devops-agent:GraphSnapshot — GraphSnapshot

**PARTIAL** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A captured application graph with named graph meaning and immutable node/edge renditions. Execution, task-link, evidence, topology, causal and configuration graphs have different evidentiary semantics.

**Identity.** Graph/capture identity + immutable rendition; endpoints are owned by the same graph snapshot.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: GRAPH_SNAPSHOT.

**Diagram symbols.** `GRAPH_SNAPSHOT`.

**Aliases.** `GRAPH_SNAPSHOT`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/kind`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/allOf/0`; `#/$defs/GraphContents`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.graph/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Snapshot(kind=TOPOLOGY) binds GraphContents with typed nodes/edges. The foundation preserves capture identity, but the original graph contract’s explicit node kinds and richer provenance/unresolved-state semantics remain incomplete.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- GraphContents.graphKind offers four broad values; named execution, task-link and configuration view meanings are not explicit variants.
- GraphNode/GraphEdge omit several source-required semantics; see their rows.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-09](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-09), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#diagram-d04--graph-snapshot-and-typed-edges)

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** An asserted possible explanation with its published disposition. A validated payload or attributed extraction; no reconstruction of private reasoning.

**Identity.** Application logical assertion/result item + retained rendition and source attribution.

**Original declaration.** Explicitly named application/native entity or application record in the model dictionary.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.assertion/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The assertion LOCAL_EVIDENCE profile retains the attributed source rendition. There is no normalized Hypothesis value or Projection variant; the generic payload and descriptor text do not establish a typed result contract.

### devops-agent:Investigation — Investigation

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An application composition over an INVESTIGATION Task, observed execution memberships, journals, phase results and historical captures.

**Identity.** Investigation continuity follows the scoped native Task identity; related execution/result/capture records retain their own identities.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: INVESTIGATION.

**Diagram symbols.** `INVESTIGATION`.

**Aliases.** `INVESTIGATION`.

**Target entities.** `Investigation`, `Execution`, `JournalRecord`, `Projection`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Investigation`; `#/$defs/TaskExecutionMembership`; `#/$defs/InvestigationResult`; `#/$defs/Snapshot`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.task/v1`; `console.investigation.result/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The aggregate is represented by Investigation plus explicitly referenced executions, journal evidence, result projections and snapshots. It is not claimed to be a native AWS Investigation response.

### devops-agent:InvestigationSnapshot — InvestigationSnapshot

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An explicit retained capture closes over task/version, membership, journal/source renditions, selected results, supporting read/coverage records and capture interval.

**Identity.** Application capture ID scoped to the investigation; later continuation does not alter an earlier capture.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SNAPSHOT.

**Diagram symbols.** `SNAPSHOT`.

**Aliases.** `Snapshot`, `SNAPSHOT`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Snapshot`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/members`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/captureStartedAt`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/captureFinishedAt`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Snapshot is a deliberate business capture with immutable versions, exact closed members, coverage and capture bounds. Its purpose is not a substitute for versioning the captured entities.

### devops-agent:JournalRecord — JournalRecord

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Published native envelope with space/execution/record ID, open recordType, createdAt and arbitrary JSON content, including unknown record types.

**Identity.** Owning scope + AgentSpace + executionId + recordId.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: JOURNAL_RECORD.

**Diagram symbols.** `JOURNAL_RECORD`.

**Aliases.** `JOURNAL_RECORD`.

**Target entities.** `JournalRecord`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/JournalRecord`; `#/$defs/JournalRecord/properties/selectedSource`; `#/$defs/JournalRecord/properties/selectedObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.journal-record/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** JournalRecord normalizes the envelope identity/type and exact source/observation references. Keeping content as retained arbitrary JSON is faithful to the native contract, not a missing universal hypothesis schema.

**NORMALIZED** · `HISTORY_REPRESENTATION` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.journal-record/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Journal history is represented by exact versions of stable Source and JournalRecord identities, with separate observation occurrences. No version-only entity is needed.

### devops-agent:RegisteredService — RegisteredService

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Provider integration registration supplies service type, accessible resources, connection configuration and native registration lifecycle; it may resolve an association.

**Identity.** Owning partition/account/Region + registered serviceId.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: REGISTERED_SERVICE.

**Diagram symbols.** `REGISTERED_SERVICE`.

**Aliases.** `REGISTERED_SERVICE`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.registered-service/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** A named registration profile retains the provider record; no normalized registration value/entity or management contract exists. It is correctly kept distinct from workload Service.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:ResourceReference — ResourceReference

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An explicitly published resource identifier or URL with namespace/type provenance and resolution state; association access does not prove incident implication.

**Identity.** Qualified external provider/resource identity or an explicit provisional reference.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: RESOURCE_REF.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESOURCE_REF`.

**Aliases.** `RESOURCE_REF`.

**Target entities.** `Resource`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/SourceIdentity`; `#/$defs/Resource/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Resource/properties/matches`; `#/$defs/GraphNode/properties/sourceIdentity`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** SourceIdentity, canonical Resource identity/matches and optional graph sourceIdentity preserve qualified or provisional references. The normalized link remains evidence-backed rather than inferred from shared workspace location.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-18](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-18), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#proposed-semantic-entities)

### devops-agent:ResultDocument — ResultDocument

**NORMALIZED** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** A versioned selected rendering/summary with phase, exact source revision, parser version and selection reason; completion-pointer absence is distinct from a retrieval gap.

**Identity.** Investigation + result phase/logical view + immutable interpretation version.

**Original declaration.** Explicitly named application/native entity or application record in the model dictionary.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Projection/allOf/2`; `#/$defs/InvestigationResult`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/ruleDigest`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.result/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** INVESTIGATION_RESULT binds InvestigationResult: selected/candidate sources, expected summary identity, phase, completion observation, availability and selection basis. Projection version/profileVersion/ruleDigest retain interpretation history.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-09](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-09), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#proposed-semantic-entities)

### devops-agent:ResumeAttempt — ResumeAttempt

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A separately observed resume submission follows a definitive decision; opening a stream does not prove tool resumption or success.

**Identity.** Application resume intent/attempt identity plus the exact prior decision context.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: RESUME_ATTEMPT.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESUME_ATTEMPT`.

**Aliases.** `RESUME_ATTEMPT`.

**Target entities.** `Command`, `ApprovalRequest`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Command`; `#/$defs/CommandAttempt`; `#/$defs/ApprovalRequest/properties/resumeCommandIds`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ResumeExecution binds exact ApprovalRequest and successful decision Command versions; Command owns its intent, lifecycle and observed effects separately from the decision. Contract semantics enforce the decision reference and ambiguous-outcome boundary.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

### devops-agent:ReviewResult — ReviewResult

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** A quality review of the published investigation record. Explicit review content/source/scope; no assumed universal native ReviewResult schema.

**Identity.** Application logical assertion/result item + retained rendition and source attribution.

**Original declaration.** Explicitly named application/native entity or application record in the model dictionary.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.assertion/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The assertion LOCAL_EVIDENCE profile retains the attributed source rendition. There is no normalized ReviewResult value or Projection variant; the generic payload and descriptor text do not establish a typed result contract.

### devops-agent:SendMessageEvents — SendMessageEvents

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `TRANSPORT_DTO` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** The native response EventStream union covers response lifecycle, content block start/delta/stop, summaries and heartbeats with variant-specific optional IDs and sequence.

**Identity.** Native request/session plus the supplied event/response/block identifiers; not a global durable event ID.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/representation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/channel`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.chat-stream/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The registered stream capture profile retains union branches and unknown variants, but its observedFields are descriptors rather than an executable normalized union schema.
