### devops-agent:SnapshotProjection — SnapshotProjection

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Pins an exact derived rendition and hash within a snapshot so a parser upgrade cannot replace the result previously reviewed.

**Identity.** Owner Snapshot version + exact Projection representation, or exact retained Source for imported legacy renditions.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SNAPSHOT_PROJECTION.

**Diagram symbols.** `SNAPSHOT_PROJECTION`.

**Aliases.** `SNAPSHOT_PROJECTION`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`, `Projection`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/members`; `#/$defs/EntityRef`; `#/$defs/Projection/properties/version`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Exact Snapshot members pin Projection versions; imported legacy rendition content is pinned as Source versions. This replaces the old projectionRevisionId without creating a separate rendition entity.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#diagram-d06--immutable-application-snapshots)

### devops-agent:SnapshotSource — SnapshotSource

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Links the snapshot to the exact retained source and supporting collection/coverage versions used in the capture.

**Identity.** Owner Snapshot version + exact referenced entity kind/ID/version.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SNAPSHOT_SOURCE.

**Diagram symbols.** `SNAPSHOT_SOURCE`.

**Aliases.** `SNAPSHOT_SOURCE`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/members`; `#/$defs/EntityRef`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Snapshot.members contains exact EntityRef values and the closure rule includes supporting ReadAttempts/evidence. Later successful collection cannot improve an older capture.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

### devops-agent:SourceObject — SourceObject

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** The stable logical object behind retained journal, task/execution, event, stream or asset representations.

**Identity.** Complete scoped source/channel/native key, independent of content digest and observing credentials.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SOURCE_OBJECT.

**Diagram symbols.** `SOURCE_OBJECT`.

**Aliases.** `SOURCE_OBJECT`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/SourceIdentity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/sourceOperation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/representation`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Source owns stable sourceId and a typed complete SourceIdentity; source operation and representation are part of its logical key. Observing credentials do not redefine native ownership.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### devops-agent:SourceObservation — SourceObservation

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** One occurrence of reading/receiving a retained source rendition, separated from the rendition so repeated unchanged polls remain observable.

**Identity.** Observation occurrence ID with exact Source version and optional native read-attempt context.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SOURCE_OBSERVATION.

**Diagram symbols.** `SOURCE_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `SOURCE_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Observation`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/readAttempt`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Observation is a separate typed entity carrying exact source, occurrence time, channel, connection and optional read attempt. Non-read channels do not manufacture native attempts.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### devops-agent:SourceRevision — SourceRevision

**NORMALIZED** · `HISTORY_REPRESENTATION` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Generic immutable retained rendition for a source object, distinct from occurrences of reading or receiving it.

**Identity.** Stable scoped Source identity + entity version; old model used revision_id and declared content hash.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: SOURCE_REVISION.

**Diagram symbols.** `SOURCE_REVISION`.

**Aliases.** `SOURCE_REVISION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/version`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/contentDigest`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/digestProfile`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Source versions preserve the retained rendition and digest profile, while Observation records repeated receipts. A changed A→B→A sequence advances normal versions instead of reviving an old rendition ID.

### devops-agent:Streamresponseblock — Stream response/block

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `TRANSPORT_DTO` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** A retained response or content block has request/session scope and supplied response ID, index, block ID, parent or sequence; unresolved IDs and interrupted fragments remain explicit.

**Aliases.** `stream response/block`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/representation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/channel`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.chat-stream/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The STREAM Source profile and receipt Observation retain the original event/fragment. There is no normalized assembled response/block model or replay cursor.

### devops-agent:Task — Task

**PARTIAL** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A native backlog work item has scoped identity, task type, status, priority, native version, optional execution pointer and external/primary-task references. The native catalog contains multiple task types.

**Identity.** Owning scope + AgentSpace + taskId.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: TASK.

**Diagram symbols.** `TASK`.

**Aliases.** `TASK`.

**Target entities.** `Investigation`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Investigation`; `#/$defs/Investigation/properties/nativeTaskVersion`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.task/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The INVESTIGATION task facet has a typed entity with identity/status/version and scoped links. EVALUATION, RELEASE_READINESS_REVIEW and RELEASE_TESTING tasks remain retained Task documents; priority and other native header fields are read through the retained source.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Only the INVESTIGATION discriminator is normalized as an application aggregate; the native Task family is broader.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### devops-agent:TaskExecutionMembership — TaskExecutionMembership

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Records a task-scoped ListExecutions request/response association because Execution does not contain a taskId and global one-task ownership is not established.

**Identity.** Scoped task/investigation + execution pair, with retained observation of the association.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a class/ER model construct: TASK_EXECUTION.

**Diagram symbols.** `TASK_EXECUTION`.

**Aliases.** `TASK_EXECUTION`.

**Target entities.** `Investigation`, `Execution`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Investigation/properties/executionMemberships`; `#/$defs/TaskExecutionMembership`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** The owned membership value links canonical Investigation and Execution IDs to an exact Observation. An Execution has no mandatory single investigation parent.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#diagram-d02--native-core-with-an-explicit-membership-table)
- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/diagrams/D02-investigation-core.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/diagrams/D02-investigation-core.mmd) — `TASK_EXECUTION`

### devops-agent:TimelineItem — TimelineItem

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** A versioned application timeline rendition with stable item identity, phase/category, evidence/record/observation clocks, provenance, availability, explicit grouping and supersession.

**Identity.** Investigation + stable logical item ID + immutable rendition.

**Original declaration.** Explicitly named application/native entity or application record in the model dictionary.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.investigation.timeline/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The dedicated source profile retains an earlier TimelineItem document. Projection.profile has no timeline variant and there is no TimelineItem value definition; its time/phase/assertion fields are not normalized.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-09](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-09), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/devops-agent/analysis/annexes/Investigation_Data_Model.md#timelineitem-contract--application-proposal)

### devops-agent:Trigger — Trigger

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Native automation resource with a condition union and open action document; documented actions can refer to custom-agent asset IDs. It is not an Asset or a console Scenario.

**Identity.** Scoped AgentSpace + triggerId.

**Original declaration.** Explicitly named application/native entity or application record in the model dictionary.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.trigger/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The trigger profile retains native identity, state, condition and action. There is no normalized automation trigger or scheduling contract.

### devops-agent:UserReference — UserReference

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Optional native journal attribution containing supplied userId and userType IAM/IDC/IDP; absent attribution does not identify an internal agent.

**Identity.** Owner JournalRecord source + supplied user identity/provider type.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `JournalRecord`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/JournalRecord/properties/selectedSource`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.journal-record/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Native userReference survives only in the retained journal Source. JournalRecord has no attribution field, and ActorRef describes a trusted acting principal with different fields and enum semantics.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- The registry’s ActorReference representation label has no normalized binding that preserves native userId/userType.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Association-scoped provider intake descriptor with webhook ID, URL and type; configuration does not prove event or incident receipt.

**Identity.** AgentSpace + association + webhookId.

**Original declaration.** Named model value, source channel, resource-family configuration or transport view; not an independently declared entity class.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.devops-agent.webhook/v1`.

## AWS FIS

### fis:ActionDependency — ActionDependency

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Directed predecessor/successor edges between actions in the same experiment; never a workflow StepRun relation.

**Identity.** Experiment identity and predecessor/successor action names.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACTION_DEPENDENCY`.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_DEPENDENCY`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/actions`; `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/actionName`; `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/startAfter`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** FISAction.startAfter is a typed owner-local predecessor-name list under Experiment.actions. It preserves the relation without an extra relationship entity or version.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd) — `ACTION_DEPENDENCY`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#2-core-execution-model)

### fis:ActionExecution — ActionExecution

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** One named runtime action in an experiment, with actual state, predecessor names and slot bindings. It has no independent stop/retry API.

**Identity.** Experiment identity plus action-map key, never catalog actionId alone.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACTION_EXECUTION`.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_EXECUTION`, `ExperimentAction`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/actions`; `#/$defs/FISAction`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.experiment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Experiment.actions binds FISAction, which preserves owner-local actionName, actionId, state, same-experiment predecessor names, slot bindings and exact observed provenance. Runtime fields that are not local indexes remain in the pinned native payload.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd) — `ACTION_EXECUTION`

### fis:ActionParameterDefinition — ActionParameterDefinition

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Parameter name plus returned description/requiredness under a catalog action. The old model explicitly refused to invent units, ranges or validation rules not supplied by a separate adapter.

**Identity.** Catalog action identity plus parameter-map key.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACTION_PARAMETER_DEFINITION`.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_PARAMETER_DEFINITION`, `ActionParameter`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.action-catalog/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The descriptor remains inside the raw catalog Source payload; there is no FisParameterDefinition value in model/schema.json despite the ownedValue label.

### fis:ActionTargetBinding — ActionTargetBinding

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Action-slot to named runtime selector relationship; the slot name is distinct from both targetName and resource identity.

**Identity.** Experiment identity, actionName and slotName.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ACTION_TARGET_BINDING`.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_TARGET_BINDING`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/targetBindings`; `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/actionName`; `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/actions`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** FISAction.targetBindings is a typed string map whose keys are slots and whose values are native selector names. The selector payload itself is retained separately and must not be reconstructed from current inventory.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Catalog action slot name and accepted native resource type, used when interpreting template and runtime bindings.

**Identity.** Catalog action plus target-slot map key.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Aliases.** `ACTION_TARGET_SLOT`, `ActionTarget`.

### fis:AlarmObservation — AlarmObservation

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Observed alarm state or transition with source time, exact alarm identity and coverage; distinct from notification receipt or FIS termination.

**Identity.** Qualified alarm plus exact observed state/history window.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ALARM_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `ALARM_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.cloudwatch.alarm/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The alarm profile retains state/history output in Source/Observation. Its observedFields are documentation hints, not a bound machine-readable alarm-result value schema.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- A typed alarm/transition evidence profile is needed before claiming normalized state/notification-proof fields; native evidence itself has a retention path.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/04_evidence_reports.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/04_evidence_reports.mmd) — `ALARM_OBSERVATION`

### fis:AssessmentCriterion — AssessmentCriterion

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Immutable threshold/recovery predicate specification with timing anchor, unit, stable-window and missing-data rules, selected at an exact revision for evaluation.

**Identity.** Criterion identity/key and immutable configuration revision.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ASSESSMENT_CRITERION`.

**Aliases.** `ASSESSMENT_CRITERION`.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepDefinition/properties/checkRole`; `#/$defs/StepDefinition/properties/required`; `#/$defs/StepDefinition/properties/inputBindings`; `#/$defs/CheckResult/properties/predicateDigest`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/criteria`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.workflow.evaluation/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** StepDefinition can mark a required criterion and its locked operation/input schema, while CheckResult pins predicateDigest and coverage. No concrete metric/recovery criterion profile specifies the old FIS timing/unit/missing-data contract, especially for evaluation without a Run.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- The broad CheckDefinition label needs a bound criterion/evaluator input contract for the prior telemetry-specific semantics; a name and arbitrary input object are insufficient.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd) — `ASSESSMENT_CRITERION`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#9-workload-assessment-model)

### fis:AssessmentEvidence — AssessmentEvidence

**NORMALIZED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Citations from an assessment result to exactly the observations accepted by its evaluator before the cutoff.

**Identity.** Owning criterion/evaluation plus exact accepted evidence reference.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE`.

**Aliases.** `ASSESSMENT_EVIDENCE`.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `StepAttempt`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/CheckResult/properties/evidenceRefs`; `#/$defs/EvidenceRef`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/inputSnapshot`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/evidenceCutoffAt`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.workflow.evaluation/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** CheckResult.evidenceRefs and Evaluation inputSnapshot/evidenceCutoffAt preserve exact citation and cutoff as owned values; no extra relationship entity is required.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### fis:AwsScope — AwsScope

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Provider ownership tuple for an experiment or scoped safety lever, distinct from application tenant scope or target-account configuration.

**Identity.** Partition, owning account and Region (with explicit null/inapplicable dimensions where appropriate).

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `AWS_SCOPE`.

**Aliases.** `AWS_SCOPE`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Resource`, `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/SourceIdentity/properties/scope`; `#/$defs/OwningScope`; `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/identity`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** SourceIdentity.scope binds OwningScope, preserving ownership independently of Workspace and Connection.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/04_evidence_reports.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/04_evidence_reports.mmd) — `AWS_SCOPE`

### fis:CatalogAction — CatalogAction

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** A catalog capability such as aws:ec2:stop-instances, describing supported inputs and target slots; distinct from every named use of that capability.

**Identity.** Native catalog ID/ARN within retrieval scope.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `CATALOG_ACTION`.

**Aliases.** `CATALOG_ACTION`, `Action`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.action-catalog/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Catalog identity, descriptions and metadata are retained under the action-catalog profile. No normalized catalog-action type is bound into the canonical model.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### fis:CloudWatchAlarm — CloudWatchAlarm

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Qualified external alarm referenced by safety configuration; its state and transition history must come from separate observations.

**Identity.** Alarm ARN or complete provider/account/Region/native alarm key.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `CLOUDWATCH_ALARM`.

**Aliases.** `CLOUDWATCH_ALARM`.

**Target entities.** `Resource`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Resource/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/SourceIdentity`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.cloudwatch.alarm/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Resource/SourceIdentity can represent the external alarm without treating it as a FIS experiment or inventing its state from a stop event.

### fis:ExecutionEvidence — ExecutionEvidence

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Retained raw execution/audit envelope and source location, with source/observation times and optional experiment/action/selector/account correlation.

**Identity.** Channel record identity/location in native owner scope; a FIS log id is an experiment ID and cannot alone deduplicate events.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `EXECUTION_EVIDENCE`.

**Aliases.** `EXECUTION_EVIDENCE`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Observation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.execution-evidence/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Source retains the exact scoped envelope/profile/version/digest and Observation retains its occurrence/channel/times. Raw execution content is intentional source evidence, not a promised normalized action-result object.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### fis:ExperimentMetricBinding — ExperimentMetricBinding

**UNMAPPED** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Exact association of an experiment and telemetry binding revision/window; several assessments can use different bindings for the same experiment.

**Identity.** Experiment identity plus exact binding revision and evaluation/observation window.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `EXPERIMENT_METRIC_BINDING`.

**Aliases.** `EXPERIMENT_METRIC_BINDING`.

**Target entities.** No claimed target entity.

**Typed paths.** No bound typed representation claimed.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** The registry targets Run and Experiment, but neither has a metric-binding association and no MetricBindingReference definition exists. Generic input JSON is not an explicit relation; the original FIS study permits an experiment assessment with no console Run.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No bound owner-local relation preserves experiment→exact telemetry definition/window independently of an orchestration Run.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#9-workload-assessment-model)

### fis:ExperimentRun — ExperimentRun

**NORMALIZED** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** One actual AWS FIS experiment with native identity, runtime action graph, selected scope, execution state and independently observed report/evidence readiness.

**Identity.** Experiment ARN, or partition/orchestrator account/Region/native experiment ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `EXPERIMENT_RUN`.

**Aliases.** `EXPERIMENT_RUN`, `Experiment`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment`; `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/selectedObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.experiment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Experiment is a first-class versioned entity with scoped native identity, selected Observation, owned FISAction values, memberships, resolution coverage and report state. Native configuration fields not indexed locally remain pinned through its Source evidence.

### fis:ExperimentTemplate — ExperimentTemplate

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Reusable native fault experiment definition, with named actions, selectors, role/configuration and account setup. Historical execution reads its captured runtime configuration rather than the latest template.

**Identity.** Owning AWS scope plus template ARN or template ID. No native template-revision ID is assumed.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `EXPERIMENT_TEMPLATE`.

**Aliases.** `EXPERIMENT_TEMPLATE`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.template/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The registered template Source preserves the native configuration and exact version. The model deliberately has no normalized template entity or template-management operation.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/01_execution.mmd) — `EXPERIMENT_TEMPLATE`
- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd) — `EXPERIMENT_TEMPLATE`

### fis:MetricBinding — MetricBinding

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Versioned workload telemetry/query specification identifying provider/scope, dimensions, statistic, unit, period, windows and normalization contract.

**Identity.** Stable binding identity plus an immutable query/normalization revision, reusable by experiment assessments.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `METRIC_BINDING`.

**Aliases.** `METRIC_BINDING`.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Scenario/properties/definition`; `#/$defs/StepDefinition/properties/inputBindings`; `#/$defs/OperationLock/properties/inputSchemaDigest`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Scenario and operation locks can pin typed operation inputs, but there is no bound MetricBinding value, schema or registered input contract showing how these required dimensions map. Arbitrary StepDefinition.inputBindings alone does not prove this mapping.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- The named MetricBinding representation has no actual typed schema/path or declared metric-query input schema.
- A targeted module-owned/profile schema should define query, units, window and normalization pins without introducing a new entity solely for versions.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd) — `METRIC_BINDING`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#9-workload-assessment-model)

### fis:MetricObservation — MetricObservation

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Numeric workload series/query result with query/binding identity, unit, time window, coverage and samples; missing values must not silently become zero.

**Identity.** Exact binding/query revision plus series and observation window.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `METRIC_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `METRIC_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/profile`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/source`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.cloudwatch.metric/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The metric profile retains these facts through Source/Observation, but observedFields are strings rather than a linked numeric sample/query schema. The registry claim of typed normalized samples is unverified.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No machine-readable metric-query/sample profile is bound; validators cannot establish units, numeric samples or window semantics from generic JsonValue.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd) — `METRIC_OBSERVATION`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#9-workload-assessment-model)

### fis:ReportArtifact — ReportArtifact

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Native report artifact descriptor containing ARN and reportType, with separately authorized content retrieval.

**Identity.** Reported artifact location plus version/digest when known, under its observed report.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `REPORT_ARTIFACT`.

**Aliases.** `REPORT_ARTIFACT`, `ExperimentReportS3Report`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`, `Source`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/FISReport/properties/artifacts`; `#/$defs/ArtifactRef`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.report/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** FISReport.artifacts binds ArtifactRef for the managed locator, version/digest and availability. The native report descriptor remains retained so its ARN and open reportType are not fabricated from a download URL.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#6-supporting-configuration-and-report-objects)

### fis:ResilienceAssessment — ResilienceAssessment

**NORMALIZED** · `DERIVED_VIEW` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Application evaluation of a FIS experiment against exact criteria and captured evidence, preserving verdict, window/cutoff and later reevaluations. This is not AWS ResilienceAssessment.

**Identity.** Application assessment identity / named projection and accepted input/rule revision.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESILIENCE_ASSESSMENT`.

**Aliases.** `RESILIENCE_ASSESSMENT`.

**Target entities.** `Projection`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Projection`; `#/$defs/Evaluation`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/inputSnapshot`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/evidenceCutoffAt`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.workflow.evaluation/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Projection profile EVALUATION binds Evaluation with exact subject, input Snapshot, evidence cutoff, verdict/finality and criterion results. It can evaluate a native Experiment without creating a workflow Run.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-09](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-09), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/05_assessments.mmd) — `RESILIENCE_ASSESSMENT`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#9-workload-assessment-model)

### fis:ResolvedTargetMembership — ResolvedTargetMembership

**PARTIAL** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Every experiment/selector membership of a resolved object, retaining full targetInformation even when no stable Resource identity can be established.

**Identity.** Owner-local membership/ingestion key with source provenance; preserve duplicate selector membership independently of deduplicated resources.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESOLVED_TARGET_MEMBERSHIP`.

**Aliases.** `RESOLVED_TARGET_MEMBERSHIP`, `ResolvedTarget`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`, `Resource`, `Observation`, `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/targetMemberships`; `#/$defs/TargetMembership`; `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/targetBindings`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.experiment/v1`; `console.fis.target-resolution/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Experiment.targetMemberships binds a typed TargetMembership with optional source/resource identity and exact Observation, but the selector relationship is not separately typed. Its only selectionKey is opaque, and the fixture combines selector and resource into workers:instance-1.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No targetName/selectorKey property binds a membership to the selector named by FISAction.targetBindings; consumers must inspect raw source or guess a selectionKey encoding.
- The model must preserve unresolved and repeated memberships without treating targetInformation as a globally unique Resource.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd) — `RESOLVED_TARGET_MEMBERSHIP`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#5-target-selectors-resolved-memberships-and-accounts)

### fis:ResourceIdentity — ResourceIdentity

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Optional normalized identity for an actually selected resource, separate from the membership and any claim that a fault succeeded.

**Identity.** Qualified provider/type/account/Region/native key; richer parent scope for types such as pod identity.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESOURCE_IDENTITY`.

**Aliases.** `RESOURCE_IDENTITY`.

**Target entities.** `Resource`, `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Resource/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/SourceIdentity`; `#/$defs/TargetMembership/properties/sourceIdentity`; `#/$defs/TargetMembership/properties/resourceId`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Resource and SourceIdentity preserve qualified/provisional identity; TargetMembership separately carries optional resourceId, identityCompleteness and source identity. No name-only join is required.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-18](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-18), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd) — `RESOURCE_IDENTITY`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#5-target-selectors-resolved-memberships-and-accounts)

### fis:ResourceType — ResourceType

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Read-only native target resource-type catalog entry, with its description and parameter metadata.

**Identity.** Native resourceType string within observed scope.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `RESOURCE_TYPE`.

**Aliases.** `RESOURCE_TYPE`, `TargetResourceType`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.resource-type/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The resource-type profile preserves the native catalog entry. A Resource instance is a different concept, so this is correctly not promoted to a Resource entity.

### fis:SafetyLever — SafetyLever

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Native account/Region control that admits or stops FIS work; an unknown local observation is not absence of the one-per-supported-scope control.

**Identity.** Provider partition/orchestrator account/Region plus native lever ID.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `SAFETY_LEVER`.

**Aliases.** `SAFETY_LEVER`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.safety-lever/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The safety-lever Source profile retains identity and state. A dedicated canonical entity and mutation capability are intentionally deferred.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### fis:SafetyLeverState — SafetyLeverState

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Native safety-control status and reason, distinct from requested state or confirmed experiment cessation.

**Identity.** Value on the exact scoped SafetyLever source representation.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.safety-lever/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The value is preserved inside the named native Source profile; no distinct normalized value schema/path is bound. This is retention coverage, not an extra entity.

### fis:StateObservation — StateObservation

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** An observed experiment/action state at a source time and collection interval, independently of the UI receiving or selecting it.

**Identity.** Scoped source state representation plus distinct observation occurrence.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `STATE_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `STATE_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/sourceTime`; `#/$defs/Observation/properties/observedAt`; `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/selectedObservation`; `#/$defs/FISAction/properties/selectedObservation`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.execution-evidence/v1`; `aws.fis.experiment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Source versions and Observation times preserve state history; Experiment and FISAction select exact observed state for their representations. Repeated receipt is not a new native lifecycle event.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### fis:StopCondition — StopCondition

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Runtime safety source/value configuration. source=none is explicit and has no alarm reference; configured conditions are not historical alarm transitions.

**Identity.** Owning experiment plus condition occurrence/source-value.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `STOP_CONDITION`.

**Aliases.** `STOP_CONDITION`, `ExperimentStopCondition`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.experiment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The complete native stopConditions array is retained by the experiment profile. No typed StopCondition value or Experiment property is declared.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- The registry ownedValue label does not identify a bound typed FisStopCondition; consumer safety inspection currently requires the Source.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

### fis:TargetResolutionObservation — TargetResolutionObservation

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Evidence that resolution ended, together with the exact selector/query, pages read, time interval and collection completeness; an empty page alone cannot prove an empty scope.

**Identity.** Bounded experiment/selector collection and its exact observations.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TARGET_RESOLUTION_OBSERVATION`.

**Aliases.** `TARGET_RESOLUTION_OBSERVATION`.

**Target entities.** `Experiment`, `Collection`, `ReadAttempt`, `Source`, `Observation`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/resolutionCoverage`; `#/$defs/Coverage`; `#/$defs/Coverage/properties/readAttempts`; `#/$defs/Collection`; `#/$defs/ReadAttempt`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.fis.target-resolution/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Experiment.resolutionCoverage and Coverage explicitly separate resolution, pagination, detail, identity and claim dimensions; exact ReadAttempt refs retain request/response coverage evidence, and the named target-resolution profile retains source-specific details.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### fis:TargetSelection — TargetSelection

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Named runtime selector retaining intended selection, resource type and original selector criteria, including a selector that resolves to no resources.

**Identity.** Experiment identity plus targetName.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TARGET_SELECTION`.

**Aliases.** `TARGET_SELECTION`, `ExperimentTarget`.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Experiment/properties/selectedObservation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.experiment/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Experiment has no typed selector collection. The full targets map remains in the exact native experiment Source. Membership rows cannot substitute for an empty or unresolved selector definition.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- The registry labels this ownedValue→Experiment without identifying an actual typed ExperimentTargetSelector path; normalization is retained-only.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/03_targeting.mmd) — `TARGET_SELECTION`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#5-target-selectors-resolved-memberships-and-accounts)

### fis:TemplateAction — TemplateAction

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Named configured action under one template, retaining capability ID, parameters, description, predecessors and target bindings.

**Identity.** Template identity plus action-map key; two names can invoke the same catalog action.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TEMPLATE_ACTION`.

**Aliases.** `TEMPLATE_ACTION`, `ExperimentTemplateAction`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.template/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** Template children are preserved in aws.fis.template/v1. FISAction is a runtime child and is not a typed template-action counterpart.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd) — `TEMPLATE_ACTION`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#3-definitions-catalog-metadata-and-templates)

### fis:TemplateBinding — TemplateBinding

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Association from a named template action slot to a selector in that same template.

**Identity.** Template identity, action name and slot name.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TEMPLATE_BINDING`.

**Aliases.** `TEMPLATE_BINDING`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.template/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The native template targets map preserves the association. No typed normalized template binding is bound outside the retained payload.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/diagrams/02_catalog_templates.mmd) — `TEMPLATE_BINDING`
- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#3-definitions-catalog-metadata-and-templates)

### fis:TemplateDependency — TemplateDependency

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `RELATIONSHIP` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Predecessor/successor relationship among named actions in one template, separate from the application workflow DAG.

**Identity.** Template identity and the two action names.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TEMPLATE_DEPENDENCY`.

**Aliases.** `TEMPLATE_DEPENDENCY`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.template/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The native startAfter array is retained under the template profile. The console StepDefinition.needs field belongs to a different DAG and cannot count as this mapping.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-06](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-06), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

- [aws/fis/analysis/report.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/report.md#3-definitions-catalog-metadata-and-templates)

### fis:TemplateTarget — TemplateTarget

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Named template selector with supplied selection mode, resource type, identifiers/tags/filters and parameters.

**Identity.** Template identity plus selector-map key.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TEMPLATE_TARGET`.

**Aliases.** `TEMPLATE_TARGET`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.template/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The selector is retained in the template payload. No normalized template-target collection is declared.

### fis:TemplateTargetAccount — TemplateTargetAccount

**RETAINED_ONLY** · `NATIVE_RESOURCE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Template-scoped native account configuration with account ID, role ARN and description. Native APIs address and mutate it separately within its template.

**Identity.** Template identity plus account ID; native child resource, not a global account directory.

**Original declaration.** Declared as a logical record/node in the original FIS ER model; this does not assert an independent AWS CRUD root.

**Diagram symbols.** `TEMPLATE_TARGET_ACCOUNT`.

**Aliases.** `TEMPLATE_TARGET_ACCOUNT`, `TargetAccountConfiguration`.

**Target entities.** `Source`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source/properties/identity`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.fis.target-account/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The target-account profile retains the complete scoped native child. There is no typed template-account owned value in the canonical schema and its administration is deferred.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-12](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-12), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

## Shared assessment workflow

### workflow:AdmissionSnapshot — AdmissionSnapshot

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Validated inputs, selected exact program, authorized bindings, observed inventory context, enabled steps and fixed required-criterion denominator at Run admission.

**Identity.** Owned by the admitted Run; now a RUN_INPUT Snapshot containing Admission.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `AdmissionSnapshot`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Run`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Run/properties/inputSnapshot`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/allOf/2/then/properties/contents`; `#/$defs/Admission`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Run.inputSnapshot pins a Snapshot whose RUN_INPUT conditional schema binds contents to Admission, including exact Scenario and Connection references, inputs, scopes, criterion set and policy/concurrency digest.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd) — `AdmissionSnapshot`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#2-publication-and-admission)

### workflow:ArtifactRef — ArtifactRef

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Durable evidence-content locator/version/media/size/digest with access resolved independently of temporary download URLs.

**Identity.** External content locator and known version/digest; owner-local reference value.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Snapshot`, `Experiment`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ArtifactRef`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/artifact`; `#/$defs/FISReport/properties/artifacts`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Source.artifact and FISReport/Asset references reuse ArtifactRef with explicit availability and nullable unknown length/version/digest.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#owned-values-and-external-references)

### workflow:BindingDeclaration — BindingDeclaration

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Named authoring declaration that requires a connection capability; resolved access is supplied by trusted admission context.

**Identity.** Scenario version and owner-local binding key.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `Binding`.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ScenarioDefinition/properties/bindings`; `#/$defs/BindingDeclaration`; `#/$defs/Admission/properties/resolvedBindings`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ScenarioDefinition.bindings binds BindingDeclaration with CONNECTION kind and capability; Admission.resolvedBindings records the exact authorized Connection context.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/scenario.schema.json](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/scenario.schema.json) — `Binding`

### workflow:CancellationIntent — CancellationIntent

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** First accepted cancellation principal/time/reason and command, monotonic and independent of actual remote termination or cleanup.

**Identity.** Singleton owned by Run; corresponding durable Command identity.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Run`, `Command`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Run/properties/cancellationIntent`; `#/$defs/CancellationIntent`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Run.cancellationIntent binds CancellationIntent, preserving actor, accepted time, reason and commandId under the Run entity version.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-15](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-15), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#5-retries-cancellation-and-finalization)

### workflow:CaptureSessionHandle — CaptureSessionHandle

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Bounded durable observation-session handle recording receiver/topic identity, starting cursor, capabilities and lease expiry before injection; an operational reference, not a seventh public aggregate.

**Identity.** Capture operation effect/handle retained by its owning attempt.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `capture-session handle`.

**Target entities.** `StepAttempt`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/externalOperations`; `#/$defs/ExternalOperationRef`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ExternalOperationRef supplies the stable owner-local handle/effect identity and exact observations; source-specific session metadata belongs to its locked adapter contract.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-15](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-15), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/adapters.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/adapters.md#complete-notification-example)

### workflow:CheckResult — CheckResult

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Sealed assertion outcome with reason, pinned predicate/evaluator, evidence and cutoff, distinct from technical execution failure.

**Identity.** Owner attempt/evaluation plus check key under exact program/inputs.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `CheckResult`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `StepAttempt`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/checkResult`; `#/$defs/CheckResult`; `#/$defs/CriterionResult`.

**Retained profiles.** `console.workflow.check-result/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** StepAttempt.checkResult binds CheckResult, and Evaluation.criteria binds CriterionResult. Verdict, predicate digest, exact evidence, cutoff, claim type and coverage are typed. Optional explanatory actual/expected values remain a profile/documentation discrepancy rather than a missing verdict entity.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd) — `CheckResult`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#owned-values-and-external-references)

### workflow:ConnectionRef — ConnectionRef

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Reference to an authorized credential/access-path owner, not copied secrets or a grant of authority.

**Identity.** Connection identity plus exact admitted version.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Connection`, `Scenario`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ResolvedBinding/properties/connection`; `#/$defs/ResolvedBinding`; `#/$defs/BindingDeclaration`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ResolvedBinding.connection is an exact Connection reference with authorization context, scopes and provider bindings; the Scenario only declares required capability.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-18](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-18), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#owned-values-and-external-references)

### workflow:CriterionDenominator — CriterionDenominator

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Exact required criterion set frozen at admission; skips/failure/cancellation do not shrink it.

**Identity.** Admission-owned requiredCriterionKeys set, interpreted with the published Scenario version.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `criterion denominator`.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`, `Run`, `Projection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Admission/properties/requiredCriterionKeys`; `#/$defs/Evaluation/properties/requiredCriterionKeys`; `#/$defs/StepDefinition/properties/checkRole`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Admission.requiredCriterionKeys and Evaluation.requiredCriterionKeys bind the preserved set; semantic checks compare it with enabled required criterion definitions.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-15](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-15), [MAP-16](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-16), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#2-publication-and-admission)

### workflow:DeploymentRef — DeploymentRef

**NORMALIZED** · `EXTERNAL_IDENTITY` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Reference to externally owned deployment inventory and the revision observed at admission; it never freezes the live deployed system.

**Identity.** Qualified deployment identity and captured inventory version in its bounded context.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Snapshot`, `Run`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Admission/properties/deploymentIdentity`; `#/$defs/Admission/properties/subjectRefs`; `#/$defs/SourceIdentity`; `#/$defs/EntityRef`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Admission preserves deploymentIdentity and exact subjectRefs; the enclosing Snapshot captures their authorized versions. There is deliberately no speculative Deployment entity.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-18](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-18), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**
