### workflow:Evidence — Evidence

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Immutable evidence retained for one Run, with optional producer attempt and explicit imported-source provenance; input/query identity, adapter/schema versions and coverage are recorded.

**Identity.** Original run-owned evidenceId; shared consolidation uses exact Source/Observation identities.

**Original declaration.** Listed among the six domain entities in workflow/model.md §1.

**Diagram symbols.** `Evidence`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `StepAttempt`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Source`; `#/$defs/Observation`; `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/evidenceRefs`; `#/$defs/Snapshot/properties/members`; `#/$defs/EvidenceRef`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Source/Observation provide versioned payload and receipt, and run inputs/attempts pin exact evidence. The original run-owned evidence manifest, producer versus consumer role and import-origin metadata are not explicitly typed in the new shared records or evidence references.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- Run has no explicit evidence manifest as claimed by the existing mapping; closure can traverse input Snapshot and selected child attempts but does not distinguish produced, consumed and imported roles.
- No declared producer/import provenance value binds the shared evidence to its old run-owned semantics. Clarify this consolidation decision or add owner-local typed citation metadata.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd) — `Evidence`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd) — `Evidence`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#6-evidence-and-assertions)

### workflow:ExecutionError — ExecutionError

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Structured technical error with code, category, safe message/provider detail and adapter retry eligibility; false checks and unresolved effects have different meanings.

**Identity.** Fact owned by an exact StepAttempt or Command attempt.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `StepAttempt`, `Command`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/outcome`; `#/$defs/CommandAttempt/properties/outcome`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** StepAttempt.outcome and CommandAttempt.outcome distinguish broad technical outcomes, but neither entity owns an ExecutionError value. ReadError is collector-owned and does not bind into workflow attempts.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No code/category/retry-eligibility/safe diagnostic value or exact error evidence contract is bound to StepAttempt/CommandAttempt despite the mapping claim.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#owned-values-and-external-references)

### workflow:ExternalOperationRef — ExternalOperationRef

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Qualified provider handle and stable originEffectKey, including an unknown native ID after a lost response, with independent remote disposition and reconciliation evidence.

**Identity.** Originating dispatch intent/effect ordinal plus provider handle once known.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `ExternalOperationRef`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `StepAttempt`, `Command`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ExternalOperationRef`; `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/externalOperations`; `#/$defs/Command/properties/effects`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** StepAttempt.externalOperations binds ExternalOperationRef with stable pre-dispatch key, optional provider/entity identity, exact observations and remote disposition.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

### workflow:FinalizationResult — FinalizationResult

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Independent cleanup outcome and unresolved-effect facts at sealing; successful criteria or local cancellation cannot imply remote quiescence.

**Identity.** Run-owned finalization state and exact finalizer/effect evidence.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `finalization result`.

**Target entities.** `Run`, `StepAttempt`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Run/properties/finalizationState`; `#/$defs/Run/properties/effectState`; `#/$defs/Run/properties/steps`; `#/$defs/ExternalOperationRef/properties/remoteDisposition`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** Run.finalizationState/effectState remain independent, and exact StepRun/StepAttempt references expose every selected finalizer and ExternalOperationRef.remoteDisposition. A duplicated finalization entity is unnecessary.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-15](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-15), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Closed object schema for authoring/admission input values, independent of one particular scenario input object.

### workflow:Observation — Observation

**PARTIAL** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Typed adapter-result content containing value, evidence IDs, collection state, claim coverage, scope/window, observed time and source; unavailable does not fabricate an empty value.

**Identity.** Owned immutable evidence/adapter-result content, not the new global Observation receipt identity.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `Source`, `Observation`, `Collection`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/Observation`; `#/$defs/Source/properties/payload`; `#/$defs/Coverage`.

**Retained profiles.** `aws.logs.query/v1`; `aws.cloudwatch.alarm/v1`; `aws.cloudwatch.metric/v1`.

**Coverage judgment.** The consolidated Observation is a typed receipt and Source retains payload; Coverage is typed elsewhere. The old adapter Observation value contract is not bound as a normalized source payload schema, and its name must not be mistaken for identical semantics.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No bound adapter-result value schema establishes value/collection/coverage/scope/window together; the existing source profiles provide field hints only.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-11](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-11), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/adapters.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/adapters.md#1-common-adapter-contract)

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Resolved immutable operation implementation/input/output schemas and capability contract retained by the published program.

**NORMALIZED** · `CONTROL_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Immutable lookup of implementation/input/output contracts by digest, separate from mutable authoring aliases.

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Bounded deliberate retry policy for eligible technical errors, distinct from transport polling, checkpoint recovery or retry-until-green.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`, `StepAttempt`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** StepDefinition.retry binds RetryPolicy; attempt identity/effect reconciliation stays in StepAttempt/ExternalOperationRef and trusted adapter semantics.

### workflow:StepAttempt — StepAttempt

**PARTIAL** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Durable attempt preserving dispatch intent/key, once-resolved inputs, checkpoint, outputs/errors, external effects and evidence while transport retries/resume retain the same attempt.

**Identity.** StepRun owner plus attemptNo; dispatch key persists before external effect.

**Original declaration.** Listed among the six domain entities in workflow/model.md §1.

**Diagram symbols.** `StepAttempt`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `StepAttempt`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepAttempt`; `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/dispatchKey`; `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/externalOperations`; `#/$defs/StepAttempt/properties/evidenceRefs`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** StepAttempt correctly preserves identity/version, dispatch key, outcome, exact evidence and ExternalOperationRef values. The prior contract also requires sealed evaluated input and durable typed resume checkpoints, but there is no corresponding input digest/reference or checkpoint value on this entity.

**Gaps / qualifications.**

- No typed resolved-input reference/digest or resume checkpoint exists on StepAttempt; StepDefinition inputBindings are authoring expressions, not per-attempt evaluated inputs.
- Runtime implementation may store private scheduler details, but the retained replay/recovery facts promised by the source mapping need an explicit contract or a declared deferred boundary.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-08](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-08), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19), [MAP-20](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-20).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd) — `StepAttempt`

### workflow:StepDefinition — StepDefinition

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** Owner-local compiled step key, operation alias, inputs, dependencies, graph phase, timeout, retry, applicability and assessment role.

**Identity.** Scenario version plus stepKey.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** `StepDefinition`.

**Aliases.** `Step`.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ScenarioDefinition/properties/steps`; `#/$defs/StepDefinition`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ScenarioDefinition.steps binds StepDefinition and its typed DAG/control fields, including input-schema-pinned extension values.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/02_owned_values.mmd) — `StepDefinition`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#owned-values-and-external-references)

### workflow:StepRun — StepRun

**NORMALIZED** · `APPLICATION_ENTITY` · `DIAGRAM_BENCHMARK`

**Previous use.** One owned step record for every compiled main/finalizer step, including skipped steps with zero attempts.

**Identity.** runId plus stepKey, with a stable canonical stepRunId.

**Original declaration.** Listed among the six domain entities in workflow/model.md §1.

**Diagram symbols.** `StepRun`.

**Aliases.** None.

**Target entities.** `StepRun`, `Run`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/StepRun`; `#/$defs/Run/properties/steps`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** StepRun retains owner identity, skip/outcome, selected exact attempt and complete known attempt references; it does not gain an independent retry/status API.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-02](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-02), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-07](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-07), [MAP-13](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-13), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/diagrams/01_entities.mmd) — `StepRun`
- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/model.md#1-entities-ownership-and-identity)

### workflow:WorkflowPolicies — WorkflowPolicies

**NORMALIZED** · `OWNED_VALUE` · `DICTIONARY_SUPPLEMENT`

**Previous use.** Independent main/finalization time budgets and deployment-concurrency policy in the authoring contract.

**Identity.** Owned policy value within Scenario version, pinned by Admission policyDigest.

**Original declaration.** An owned value, native nested shape, reference or control construct; not an additional original domain entity.

**Diagram symbols.** Supplementary dictionary/control concept.

**Aliases.** `Policies`.

**Target entities.** `Scenario`, `Snapshot`.

**Typed paths.** `#/$defs/ScenarioDefinition/properties/policies`; `#/$defs/WorkflowPolicies`; `#/$defs/Admission/properties/policyDigest`.

**Retained profiles.** None claimed.

**Coverage judgment.** ScenarioDefinition.policies binds WorkflowPolicies with corresponding budgets and QUEUE/REJECT deployment policy; the value shares its Scenario version.

**Rules.** [MAP-01](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-01), [MAP-03](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-03), [MAP-04](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-04), [MAP-05](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-05), [MAP-17](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-17), [MAP-19](../MAPPING_RULES.md#map-19).

**Source evidence.**

- [aws/fis/analysis/workflow/scenario.schema.json](https://github.com/lyrel-dev/frontier-agents/blob/d926a81c584b6b69f41bfb9641532c4f8f2d7cb3/aws/fis/analysis/workflow/scenario.schema.json) — `Policies`

## Selected DevOps SDK shapes — separate population

The 52 returned/nested type declarations below overlap the native concepts already discussed; they are transport/schema coverage, not 52 additional domain entities.

| Native type | Kind | Coverage | Interpretation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `AgentSpace` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | NORMALIZED | AgentSpace provides a typed scoped entity and association collection with exact selected observation. Peripheral provider configuration remains in its retained Source. |
| `ApprovalAction` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The console’s typed DecideApproval/ResumeExecution commands express narrower intent, but no equivalent normalized ApprovalAction model value preserves its native invocation members. |
| `ApprovalPattern` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Approval context and digest are modeled, but the tool/argumentPins shape has no model definition or direct typed member. It remains in retained source content for adapter validation. |
| `Asset` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | NORMALIZED | Asset has stable identity and ordinary entity versions with separate nativeVersion, exact source and files. Provider metadata stays retained; no inferred investigation-to-asset relation is introduced. |
| `AssetContent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssetFile` | OWNED_VALUE | NORMALIZED | AssetFile is a typed owned path/source/artifact value under the selected Asset representation. Raw body union and native file metadata remain source-backed, and the Asset nativeVersion supplies ownership. |
| `AssetFileBody` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssetFileContent` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssetFileSummary` | TRANSPORT_DTO | NORMALIZED | AssetFile is a typed owned path/source/artifact value under the selected Asset representation. Raw body union and native file metadata remain source-backed, and the Asset nativeVersion supplies ownership. |
| `AssetSourceUrlContent` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssetTypeSummary` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssetVersionMetadata` | TRANSPORT_DTO | NORMALIZED | Asset.nativeVersion plus an exact Source and ordinary Asset entity version represent the selected native content version. Provider created/updated metadata stays in Source; this is not a separate Version entity. |
| `AssetZipContent` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native asset transport/catalog/content shape is retained by the asset profile. Asset/AssetFile normalize identity and content references, not these provider-specific unions and catalog rows. |
| `AssistantMessageBlock` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native message/content union remains in Source payload. No normalized conversation Message or block hierarchy is declared. |
| `Association` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | NORMALIZED | AgentAssociation is a typed owned value in each AgentSpace version with associationId, serviceId, configurationKind, optional registration identity and exact source. Demotion to an owned value preserves its source identity; native configuration remains Source-backed. |
| `ChatExecution` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | The conversation profile retains ChatExecution output; no normalized chat-execution view or conversation history model is defined. |
| `Execution` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | NORMALIZED | Execution preserves identity, parent identity, state, subtask and exact observation. Optional native agent/session metadata remains source-backed rather than guessed. |
| `Goal` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | RETAINED_ONLY | The named goal profile preserves native identity and content/lifecycle in Source; goal authoring/scheduling and a normalized goal entity/value are explicitly deferred. |
| `GoalContent` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native shape is retained under its source profile. Profile observedFields describe source vocabulary; they are not an executable normalized JSON Schema for this native payload. |
| `JournalRecord` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | NORMALIZED | JournalRecord normalizes the envelope identity/type and exact source/observation references. Keeping content as retained arbitrary JSON is faithful to the native contract, not a missing universal hypothesis schema. |
| `Message` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native message/content union remains in Source payload. No normalized conversation Message or block hierarchy is declared. |
| `PendingMessage` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Pending message data remains inside the conversation Source profile; queue membership is not converted into durable normalized conversation history. |
| `PrivateConnectionSummary` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | The private-connection profile retains identity and lifecycle evidence; provisioning and certificate-management normalization remain deferred. |
| `Recommendation` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | RETAINED_ONLY | A separate source profile preserves the native recommendation. The consolidated Recommendation entity belongs to the Resilience finding model and must not be counted as normalizing this DevOps resource. |
| `RecommendationContent` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native shape is retained under its source profile. Profile observedFields describe source vocabulary; they are not an executable normalized JSON Schema for this native payload. |
| `ReferenceInput` | OWNED_VALUE | NORMALIZED | The essential external-system/reference/URL/association linkage has a typed ExternalReference binding. Native input bounds and optional title remain source/adapter concerns; this is not full SDK-schema equivalence. |
| `ReferenceOutput` | OWNED_VALUE | NORMALIZED | The essential external-system/reference/URL/association linkage has a typed ExternalReference binding. Native input bounds and optional title remain source/adapter concerns; this is not full SDK-schema equivalence. |
| `RegisteredService` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | RETAINED_ONLY | A named registration profile retains the provider record; no normalized registration value/entity or management contract exists. It is correctly kept distinct from workload Service. |
| `SendMessageContentBlockDelta` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageContentBlockDeltaEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageContentBlockStartEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageContentBlockStopEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageContext` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageEvents` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageHeartbeatEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageJsonDelta` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageResponseCompletedEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageResponseCreatedEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageResponseFailedEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageResponseInProgressEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageSummaryEvent` | TRANSPORT_DTO | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageTextDelta` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `SendMessageUsageInfo` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native conversation/context or stream union value is retained verbatim by the named profile; there is no corresponding normalized message/block/event shape. |
| `ServiceConfiguration` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | AgentAssociation normalizes configurationKind, while the native tagged configuration union remains Source payload with descriptor-level source-shape hints. |
| `Task` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | PARTIAL | The INVESTIGATION task facet has a typed entity with identity/status/version and scoped links. EVALUATION, RELEASE_READINESS_REVIEW and RELEASE_TESTING tasks remain retained Task documents; priority and other native header fields are read through the retained source. |
| `TaskFilter` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Exact filters/query and request envelopes are retained, but CollectionQuery.filters permits arbitrary JSON and does not normalize the native TaskFilter vocabulary/constraints. |
| `Trigger` | NATIVE_RESOURCE | RETAINED_ONLY | The trigger profile retains native identity, state, condition and action. There is no normalized automation trigger or scheduling contract. |
| `TriggerCondition` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native shape is retained under its source profile. Profile observedFields describe source vocabulary; they are not an executable normalized JSON Schema for this native payload. |
| `UsageMetric` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The account-usage profile retains typed native counter descriptions inside Source. The model has no normalized UsageMetric/account-usage value and does not conflate it with workload status. |
| `UserMessageBlock` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The native message/content union remains in Source payload. No normalized conversation Message or block hierarchy is declared. |
| `UserReference` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | Native userReference survives only in the retained journal Source. JournalRecord has no attribution field, and ActorRef describes a trusted acting principal with different fields and enum semantics. |
| `Webhook` | OWNED_VALUE | RETAINED_ONLY | The webhook profile retains the native descriptor. No normalized webhook value or intake delivery entity is defined. |
